## Gate results 2026-09-08

Run with `GRADLE_ARGS='-Pcompose.desktop.packaging.checkJdkVendor=false' scripts/verify.sh full` on Homebrew JDK 21.

| Gate | Result | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| desktop | PASS | `:domain:jvmTest :apps:desktop:compileKotlin :apps:desktop:packageDmg` |
| android | PASS | `:apps:droid:assembleDebug`; needed a `META-INF/LICENSE.md` packaging exclude for Bouncy Castle 1.85 |
| ios | PASS | `:iosdi` debug frameworks for iosSimulatorArm64 and iosArm64 |
| embedded | PASS | `-Pembedded.enabled=true :apps:embedded:linkDebugExecutableLinuxArm64` |

Ceilings found while bumping, all waiting on the Kotlin/Compose upgrade:

- Gradle stops at 9.5.1. 9.6.0 removed `org.gradle.api.problems.internal.InternalProblems`, which AGP 8.x needs to apply `com.android.internal.application`.
- Coil stops at 3.3.0. From 3.4.0 up its klibs are built with a Kotlin the 2.2.10 native compiler cannot read, so the iOS gate fails in the KLIB resolver even though the JVM side compiles.
- Bouncy Castle stops at 1.85. bcprov has a 1.85.2 patch but bcpg, bcutil and bcpkix do not.

## Gate results 2026-09-08 (after the Kotlin/Compose/Koin upgrade)

`GRADLE_ARGS='-Pcompose.desktop.packaging.checkJdkVendor=false' scripts/verify.sh full` on Homebrew JDK 21: desktop, android, ios and embedded all PASS, with no KMP dependency-resolution warnings.

Landed: Kotlin 2.4.20, Compose Multiplatform 1.11.1 (non-embedded), Koin 4.2.2, Coil 3.5.0.

The Compose and Koin forks did **not** need rebuilding for the Kotlin bump: the 2.4.20 compiler reads the klibs they were built with under 2.2.10. Koin was re-cut anyway to reach 4.2.2 (`../forks/koin` branch `sa_linux_4.2.2`, published to `~/.m2` beside 4.1.2). The Compose fork is untouched and still publishes `9999.0.0-SNAPSHOT` from the 1.10 line, so embedded runs 1.10 while every other target runs 1.11.1.

AGP 8.12.3 is now the single ceiling holding back the rest:

- Compose Multiplatform 1.12.0 requires AGP 9.1.0 and compileSdk 37.
- Coil 3.6.x requires compileSdk 37; AGP 8.12.3 caps at 36.
- Gradle 9.6.0+ removed the internal Problems API that AGP 8.x applies its plugin through.

`iosX64` was dropped from every module: Coil from 3.5.0 and Compose from 1.11 no longer publish it. `macosX64` was deliberately kept and restored in the Koin fork, because `apps/desktop` builds the Intel-Mac BLE dylib from that target. Kotlin 2.3.20 rejects `macosX64()` as removed; 2.4.20 still accepts it, so the Koin fork is pinned to 2.4.20.
